Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in bonding theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 365 and the invariant I(A,B) = 843 mod 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation.
